- **recibos-e-garantia**: Turn a photo of a Portuguese receipt into structured, validated, categorised data — offline. Decodes the mandatory QR code (Portaria 195/2020), reconciles the arithmetic against the AT's own worked examples, flags missing NIFs and unreadable fields, refuses business invoices, computes consumer-guarantee deadlines under DL 84/2021, writes a sortable spreadsheet, and exports guarantee reminders as iCal. Use when the user mentions faturas, recibos, talões, e-Fatura, QR code, ATCUD, garantia, prazo de garantia, devolução, or wants receipts organised for IRS.
